Reimplement `validateCreate(doc)` on top of `validators` with exactly its current acceptance behavior: `{ok: true}` when valid; otherwise `{ok: false, errors}` mapping each invalid field name to the string `\"invalid\"`. A missing (undefined) `name` or `qty` is invalid; `tags` absent is valid; other fields are ignored.\n\n3. `src/router.js` — add `\"PATCH /items/:id\"`; requests are `{method, path, body}`. If any provided field fails its validator, respond `{status: 400, body: {error: 'invalid patch', fields}}` with `fields` mapping each invalid field name to `true`. If `body` is not a plain object (null, array, or primitive), respond `{status: 400, body: {error: 'invalid patch', fields: {}}}`. Validation happens before the store lookup: an invalid patch on an unknown id is 400, not 404. If the id does not exist and the patch was valid, respond `{status: 404, body: {error: 'not found'}}`; `dispatch` must catch `NotFoundError` raised by handlers and return that response instead of throwing. On success respond `{status: 200, body: <updated doc>}`; an empty patch `{}` is valid and returns the current doc.
